     1 import React from 'react';
     2 import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
     3 import {
     4   ScatterChart,
     5   Scatter,
     6   XAxis,
     7   YAxis,
     8   CartesianGrid,
     9   Tooltip,
    10   ResponsiveContainer,
    11   Cell,
    12   ReferenceLine,
    13   Label,
    14 } from 'recharts';
    15 import type { IntelligenceEvent } from '@/types/intelligence';
    16 
    17 interface OpportunityRadarProps {
    18   events: IntelligenceEvent[];
    19   onEventClick?: (eventId: string) => void;
    20 }
    21 
    22 // 颜色映射
    23 const urgencyColorMap: Record<string, string> = {
    24   critical: '#ef4444',
    25   high: '#f97316',
    26   medium: '#eab308',
    27   low: '#22c55e',
    28 };
    29 
    30 const CustomTooltip = ({ active, payload }: any) => {
    31   if (active && payload && payload.length) {
    32     const data = payload[0].payload;
    33     return (
    34       <div className="bg-card border border-border rounded-lg p-3 shadow-lg max-w-[240px]">
    35         <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-foreground mb-1">{data.name}</p>
    36         <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground mb-1.5">{data.market}</p>
    37         <div className="space-y-1 text-[10px]">
    38           <div className="flex justify-between">
    39             <span className="text-muted-foreground">影响度</span>
    40             <span className="font-medium">{data.impact}%</span>
    41           </div>
    42           <div className="flex justify-between">
    43             <span className="text-muted-foreground">紧急度</span>
    44             <span className="font-medium">{data.urgencyLabel}</span>
    45           </div>
    46           <div className="flex justify-between">
    47             <span className="text-muted-foreground">置信度</span>
    48             <span className="font-medium">{data.confidenceScore}</span>
    49           </div>
    50         </div>
    51       </div>
    52     );
    53   }
    54   return null;
    55 };
    56 
    57 const OpportunityRadar: React.FC<OpportunityRadarProps> = ({ events, onEventClick }) => {
    58   const urgencyScoreMap: Record<string, number> = {
    59     critical: 90,
    60     high: 70,
    61     medium: 45,
    62     low: 20,
    63   };
    64 
    65   const urgencyLabelMap: Record<string, string> = {
    66     critical: '紧急',
    67     high: '重要',
    68     medium: '一般',
    69     low: '低',
    70   };
    71 
    72   const chartData = events.map((e) => ({
    73     x: e.impactScore,
    74     y: urgencyScoreMap[e.urgency] || 50,
    75     name: e.title.length > 20 ? e.title.slice(0, 20) + '...' : e.title,
    76     fullName: e.title,
    77     eventId: e.id,
    78     market: e.markets.map((m) => {
    79       const names: Record<string, string> = {
    80         china: '中国', japan: '日本', korea: '韩国',
    81         southeast_asia: '东南亚', usa: '美国',
    82       };
    83       return names[m] || m;
    84     }).join(' · '),
    85     impact: e.impactScore,
    86     urgencyLabel: urgencyLabelMap[e.urgency] || e.urgency,
    87     confidenceScore: e.confidenceScore,
    88     urgency: e.urgency,
    89   }));
    90 
    91   return (
    92     <Card className="p-4">
    93       <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
    94         <div>
    95           <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">机会/风险雷达</h3>
    96           <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">高影响×高紧急 = 立即行动</p>
    97         </div>
    98       </div>
    99 
   100       <div className="h-[300px] w-full">
   101         <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
   102           <ScatterChart margin={{ top: 10, right: 10, bottom: 30, left: 30 }}>
   103             <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="hsl(var(--border))" opacity={0.5} />
   104             <XAxis
   105               type="number"
   106               dataKey="x"
   107               name="影响度"
   108               domain={[0, 100]}
   109               tick={{ fontSize: 10 }}
   110               stroke="hsl(var(--muted-foreground))"
   111             >
   112               <Label
   113                 value="影响度 →"
   114                 position="bottom"
   115                 offset={10}
   116                 style={{ fontSize: 11, fill: 'hsl(var(--muted-foreground))' }}
   117               />
   118             </XAxis>
   119             <YAxis
   120               type="number"
   121               dataKey="y"
   122               name="紧急度"
   123               domain={[0, 100]}
   124               tick={{ fontSize: 10 }}
   125               stroke="hsl(var(--muted-foreground))"
   126             >
   127               <Label
   128                 value="紧急度 →"
   129                 position="left"
   130                 angle={-90}
   131                 offset={10}
   132                 style={{ fontSize: 11, fill: 'hsl(var(--muted-foreground))' }}
   133               />

   137             <ReferenceLine y={50} stroke="hsl(var(--border))" strokeDasharray="5 5" />
   138             <Scatter
   139               data={chartData}

    48 // 计算多源置信度评分
    49 function calcConfidenceScore(refs: Reference[]): number {
    50   if (!refs.length) return 45;
    51   const sourceCount = Math.min(refs.length, 8);
    52   const avgReliability = refs.reduce((s, _r) => s + 85, 0) / refs.length; // 搜索结果默认可靠度85
    53   const consistency = sourceCount >= 3 ? 90 : sourceCount >= 2 ? 75 : 60;
    54   return Math.round(sourceCount / 8 * 30 + avgReliability * 0.4 + consistency * 0.3);
    55 }
